```
   948→        &>"${LOG_DIR}/sysbox-mgr.log" &
   949→    SYSBOX_MGR_PID=$!
   950→    echo "$SYSBOX_MGR_PID" > "${SYSBOX_RUN_DIR}/sysbox-mgr.pid"
   951→    STARTED_PIDS+=("$SYSBOX_MGR_PID")
   952→    wait_for_file "${SYSBOX_RUN_DIR}/sysmgr.sock" "sysbox-mgr" 30 || cleanup
   953→    kill -0 "$SYSBOX_MGR_PID" 2>/dev/null || { echo "sysbox-mgr exited unexpectedly" >&2; cleanup; }
   954→    echo "  sysbox-mgr ready (pid ${SYSBOX_MGR_PID})"
   955→
   956→    echo "Starting sysbox-fs..."
   957→    "${BIN_DIR}/sysbox-fs" --run-dir "${SYSBOX_RUN_DIR}" --mountpoint "${SYSBOX_DATA_DIR}" \
   958→        &>"${LOG_DIR}/sysbox-fs.log" &
   959→    SYSBOX_FS_PID=$!
   960→    echo "$SYSBOX_FS_PID" > "${SYSBOX_RUN_DIR}/sysbox-fs.pid"
   961→    STARTED_PIDS+=("$SYSBOX_FS_PID")
   962→    wait_for_file "${SYSBOX_RUN_DIR}/sysfs.sock" "sysbox-fs" 30 || cleanup
   963→    kill -0 "$SYSBOX_FS_PID" 2>/dev/null || { echo "sysbox-fs exited unexpectedly" >&2; cleanup; }
   964→    echo "  sysbox-fs ready (pid ${SYSBOX_FS_PID})"
   965→
   966→    # --- 2. Create bridge ---
   967→    if ! ip link show "$BRIDGE" &>/dev/null; then
   968→        echo "Creating bridge ${BRIDGE}..."
   969→        ip link add "$BRIDGE" type bridge
   970→        ip addr add "$DOCKYARD_BRIDGE_CIDR" dev "$BRIDGE"
   971→        ip link set "$BRIDGE" up
   972→    else
   973→        echo "Bridge ${BRIDGE} already exists"
   974→    fi
   975→
   976→    # Enable IP forwarding
   977→    sysctl -w net.ipv4.ip_forward=1 >/dev/null
   978→
   979→    # Bridge rules (idempotent)
   980→    iptables -C FORWARD -i "$BRIDGE" -o "$BRIDGE" -j ACCEPT 2>/dev/null ||
   981→        iptables -I FORWARD -i "$BRIDGE" -o "$BRIDGE" -j ACCEPT
   982→    iptables -C FORWARD -i "$BRIDGE" ! -o "$BRIDGE" -j ACCEPT 2>/dev/null ||
   983→        iptables -I FORWARD -i "$BRIDGE" ! -o "$BRIDGE" -j ACCEPT
   984→    iptables -C FORWARD -o "$BRIDGE" -m conntrack --ctstate RELATED,ESTABLISHED -j ACCEPT 2>/dev/null ||
   985→        iptables -I FORWARD -o "$BRIDGE" -m conntrack --ctstate RELATED,ESTABLISHED -j ACCEPT
   986→    iptables -t nat -C POSTROUTING -s "$DOCKYARD_FIXED_CIDR" ! -o "$BRIDGE" -j MASQUERADE 2>/dev/null ||
   987→        iptables -t nat -I POSTROUTING -s "$DOCKYARD_FIXED_CIDR" ! -o "$BRIDGE" -j MASQUERADE
   988→
   989→    # Pool rules
   990→    iptables -C FORWARD -s "$DOCKYARD_POOL_BASE" -j ACCEPT 2>/dev/null ||
   991→        iptables -I FORWARD -s "$DOCKYARD_POOL_BASE" -j ACCEPT
   992→    iptables -C FORWARD -d "$DOCKYARD_POOL_BASE" -j ACCEPT 2>/dev/null ||
   993→        iptables -I FORWARD -d "$DOCKYARD_POOL_BASE" -j ACCEPT
   994→    iptables -t nat -C POSTROUTING -s "$DOCKYARD_POOL_BASE" -j MASQUERADE 2>/dev/null ||
   995→        iptables -t nat -I POSTROUTING -s "$DOCKYARD_POOL_BASE" -j MASQUERADE
   996→
   997→    # --- 3. Start containerd ---
   998→    echo "Starting containerd..."
   999→    "${BIN_DIR}/containerd" \
  1000→        --root "$DOCKER_DATA/containerd" \
  1001→        --state "${RUN_DIR}/containerd" \
  1002→        --address "$CONTAINERD_SOCKET" \
  1003→        &>"${LOG_DIR}/containerd.log" &
  1004→    CONTAINERD_PID=$!
  1005→    echo "$CONTAINERD_PID" > "${RUN_DIR}/containerd.pid"
  1006→    STARTED_PIDS+=("$CONTAINERD_PID")
  1007→
  1008→    wait_for_file "$CONTAINERD_SOCKET" "containerd" || cleanup
  1009→    echo "  containerd ready (pid ${CONTAINERD_PID})"
  1010→
  1011→    # --- 4. Start dockerd ---
  1012→    echo "Starting dockerd..."
  1013→    "${BIN_DIR}/dockerd" \
  1014→        --config-file "${ETC_DIR}/daemon.json" \
  1015→        --containerd "$CONTAINERD_SOCKET" \
  1016→        --data-root "$DOCKER_DATA" \
  1017→        --exec-root "$RUN_DIR" \
  1018→        --pidfile "${RUN_DIR}/dockerd.pid" \
  1019→        --bridge "$BRIDGE" \
  1020→        --fixed-cidr "$DOCKYARD_FIXED_CIDR" \
  1021→        --default-address-pool "base=${DOCKYARD_POOL_BASE},size=${DOCKYARD_POOL_SIZE}" \
  1022→        --host "unix://${DOCKER_SOCKET}" \
  1023→        --iptables=false \
  1024→        --group "${INSTANCE_GROUP}" \
  1025→        &>"${LOG_DIR}/dockerd.log" &
  1026→    DOCKERD_PID=$!
  1027→    STARTED_PIDS+=("$DOCKERD_PID")
  1028→
  1029→    wait_for_file "$DOCKER_SOCKET" "dockerd" 30 || cleanup
  1030→    echo "  dockerd ready (pid ${DOCKERD_PID})"
  1031→
  1032→    echo "=== All daemons started ==="
  1033→    echo "Run: DOCKER_HOST=unix://${DOCKER_SOCKET} docker ps"
  1034→}
  1035→
  1036→cmd_stop() {
  1037→    require_root
  1038→
  1039→    # Reverse startup order: dockerd -> containerd -> sysbox
  1040→    stop_daemon dockerd "${RUN_DIR}/dockerd.pid" 20
  1041→    stop_daemon containerd "${RUN_DIR}/containerd.pid" 10
  1042→    stop_daemon sysbox-fs "${SYSBOX_RUN_DIR}/sysbox-fs.pid" 10
  1043→    stop_daemon sysbox-mgr "${SYSBOX_RUN_DIR}/sysbox-mgr.pid" 10
  1044→    rm -rf "$SYSBOX_RUN_DIR"
  1045→
  1046→    # Clean up sockets
  1047→    rm -f "$DOCKER_SOCKET" "$CONTAINERD_SOCKET"
  1048→
  1049→    # Remove iptables rules (bridge)
  1050→    iptables -D FORWARD -i "$BRIDGE" -o "$BRIDGE" -j ACCEPT 2>/dev/null || true
  1051→    iptables -D FORWARD -i "$BRIDGE" ! -o "$BRIDGE" -j ACCEPT 2>/dev/null || true
  1052→    iptables -D FORWARD -o "$BRIDGE" -m conntrack --ctstate RELATED,ESTABLISHED -j ACCEPT 2>/dev/null || true
  1053→    iptables -t nat -D POSTROUTING -s "$DOCKYARD_FIXED_CIDR" ! -o "$BRIDGE" -j MASQUERADE 2>/dev/null || true
  1054→    # Remove iptables rules (pool)
  1055→    iptables -D FORWARD -s "$DOCKYARD_POOL_BASE" -j ACCEPT 2>/dev/null || true
  1056→    iptables -D FORWARD -d "$DOCKYARD_POOL_BASE" -j ACCEPT 2>/dev/null || true
  1057→    iptables -t nat -D POSTROUTING -s "$DOCKYARD_POOL_BASE" -j MASQUERADE 2>/dev/null || true
  1058→
  1059→    # Remove bridge
  1060→    if ip link show "$BRIDGE" &>/dev/null; then
  1061→        ip link set "$BRIDGE" down
  1062→        ip link delete "$BRIDGE"
  1063→        echo "Bridge ${BRIDGE} removed"
  1064→    fi
  1065→
  1066→    # Remove leftover user-defined network bridges from the pool range
  1067→    cleanup_pool_bridges
  1068→
  1069→    echo "=== All daemons stopped ==="
  1070→}
```

```
  1281→
  1282→# ── Verify ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  1283→# Smoke-tests a running dockyard instance: service, socket, API, containers,
  1284→# outbound networking, and Docker-in-Docker (sysbox).
  1285→# Exits 0 only when every check passes.
  1286→
  1287→cmd_verify() {
  1288→    local _p=0 _f=0
  1289→    local _d="${BIN_DIR}/docker"
  1290→    local _s="unix://${DOCKER_SOCKET}"
  1291→    local out
  1292→
  1293→    _pass() { echo "  PASS: $1"; _p=$((_p + 1)); }
  1294→    _fail() { echo "  FAIL: $1 — $2" >&2; _f=$((_f + 1)); }
  1295→
  1296→    echo "=== dockyard verify: ${SERVICE_NAME} ==="
  1297→    echo ""
  1298→
  1299→    # 1. systemd service active
  1300→    if systemctl is-active --quiet "${SERVICE_NAME}"; then
  1301→        _pass "systemd service ${SERVICE_NAME} active"
  1302→    else
  1303→        _fail "systemd service" "${SERVICE_NAME} is $(systemctl is-active "${SERVICE_NAME}" 2>/dev/null || echo unknown)"
  1304→    fi
  1305→
  1306→    # 2. docker socket exists
  1307→    if [ -S "${DOCKER_SOCKET}" ]; then
  1308→        _pass "docker socket exists"
  1309→    else
  1310→        _fail "docker socket" "${DOCKER_SOCKET} not found"
  1311→    fi
  1312→
  1313→    # 3. docker info (API reachable)
  1314→    if DOCKER_HOST="$_s" "$_d" info >/dev/null 2>&1; then
  1315→        _pass "docker info (API reachable)"
  1316→    else
  1317→        out=$(DOCKER_HOST="$_s" "$_d" info 2>&1 | tail -2)
  1318→        _fail "docker info" "$out"
  1319→    fi
  1320→
  1321→    # 4. basic container run
  1322→    out=$(DOCKER_HOST="$_s" "$_d" run --rm alpine echo verify-ok 2>&1)
  1323→    if echo "$out" | grep -q "verify-ok"; then
  1324→        _pass "container run (alpine echo)"
  1325→    else
  1326→        _fail "container run" "$out"
  1327→    fi
  1328→
  1329→    # 5. outbound networking
  1330→    if DOCKER_HOST="$_s" "$_d" run --rm alpine ping -c3 -W2 1.1.1.1 >/dev/null 2>&1; then
  1331→        _pass "outbound networking (ping 1.1.1.1)"
  1332→    else
  1333→        _fail "outbound networking" "ping 1.1.1.1 failed from container"
  1334→    fi
  1335→
  1336→    # 6. Docker-in-Docker via sysbox
  1337→    local cname="dockyard-verify-$$"
  1338→    DOCKER_HOST="$_s" "$_d" rm -f "$cname" >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
  1339→    if DOCKER_HOST="$_s" "$_d" run -d --name "$cname" docker:26.1-dind >/dev/null 2>&1; then
  1340→        local ready=false i
  1341→        for i in $(seq 1 30); do
  1342→            if DOCKER_HOST="$_s" "$_d" exec "$cname" docker info >/dev/null 2>&1; then
  1343→                ready=true
  1344→                break
  1345→            fi
  1346→            sleep 2
  1347→        done
  1348→        if $ready; then
  1349→            # Preload alpine from host cache to avoid Docker Hub rate limits.
  1350→            if [ -f /var/tmp/alpine.tar ]; then
  1351→                DOCKER_HOST="$_s" "$_d" exec -i "$cname" docker load < /var/tmp/alpine.tar >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
  1352→            fi
  1353→            out=$(DOCKER_HOST="$_s" "$_d" exec "$cname" docker run --rm alpine echo dind-ok 2>&1)
  1354→            if echo "$out" | grep -q "dind-ok"; then
  1355→                _pass "Docker-in-Docker (inner container via sysbox)"
  1356→            else
  1357→                _fail "DinD inner container" "$out"
  1358→            fi
  1359→        else
  1360→            _fail "DinD" "inner dockerd not ready after 60s"
  1361→        fi
  1362→        DOCKER_HOST="$_s" "$_d" rm -f "$cname" >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
  1363→    else
  1364→        out=$(DOCKER_HOST="$_s" "$_d" run --name "$cname" docker:26.1-dind 2>&1 | head -3)
  1365→        DOCKER_HOST="$_s" "$_d" rm -f "$cname" >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
  1366→        _fail "DinD" "could not start docker:26.1-dind — $out"
  1367→    fi
  1368→
  1369→    echo ""
  1370→    if [ "$_f" -eq 0 ]; then
  1371→        echo "All ${_p} checks passed."
  1372→    else
  1373→        echo "${_p} passed, ${_f} failed."
  1374→    fi
  1375→    return "$_f"
  1376→}
  1377→
  1378→# ── Usage ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  1379→
  1380→usage() {
  1381→    cat <<'EOF'
  1382→Usage: ./dockyard.sh <command> [options]
  1383→
  1384→Commands:
  1385→  gen-env     Generate a conflict-free dockyard.env config file
  1386→  create      Download binaries, install config, set up systemd, start daemon
  1387→  enable      Install systemd service for this instance
  1388→  disable     Remove systemd service for this instance
  1389→  start       Start daemons manually (without systemd)
  1390→  stop        Stop manually started daemons
  1391→  status      Show instance status
  1392→  verify      Smoke-test a running instance (service, containers, DinD, networking)
  1393→  destroy     Stop and remove everything
  1394→
  1395→All commands except gen-env require a config file:
  1396→  1. $DOCKYARD_ENV (if set)
  1397→  2. ./dockyard.env (in current directory)
  1398→  3. ../etc/dockyard.env (relative to script — for installed copy)
  1399→  4. $DOCKYARD_ROOT/etc/dockyard.env
  1400→
  1401→Examples:
  1402→  ./dockyard.sh gen-env
  1403→  sudo ./dockyard.sh create
  1404→  sudo ./dockyard.sh create --no-systemd --no-start
  1405→  sudo ./dockyard.sh start
  1406→  sudo ./dockyard.sh stop
  1407→  ./dockyard.sh status
  1408→  sudo ./dockyard.sh verify
  1409→  sudo ./dockyard.sh destroy
  1410→  sudo ./dockyard.sh destroy --keep-data   # preserve container data
  1411→
  1412→  # Multiple instances
  1413→  DOCKYARD_DOCKER_PREFIX=test_ DOCKYARD_ROOT=/test ./dockyard.sh gen-env
  1414→  DOCKYARD_ENV=./dockyard.env sudo -E ./dockyard.sh create
  1415→EOF
  1416→    exit 0
  1417→}
  1418→
  1419→gen_env_usage() {
  1420→    cat <<'EOF'
  1421→Usage: ./dockyard.sh gen-env [OPTIONS]
  1422→
  1423→Generate a dockyard.env config file with randomized, conflict-free networks.
  1424→
  1425→Options:
  1426→  --nocheck     Skip all conflict checks
  1427→  -h, --help    Show this help
  1428→
  1429→Output: ./dockyard.env (or $DOCKYARD_ENV if set). Errors if file exists.
  1430→
  1431→Override any variable via environment:
  1432→  DOCKYARD_ROOT           Installation root (default: /dockyard)
  1433→  DOCKYARD_DOCKER_PREFIX  Prefix for bridge/service (default: dy_)
  1434→  DOCKYARD_BRIDGE_CIDR    Bridge IP/mask (default: random from 172.16.0.0/12)
  1435→  DOCKYARD_FIXED_CIDR     Container subnet (default: derived from bridge)
  1436→  DOCKYARD_POOL_BASE      Address pool base (default: random from 172.16.0.0/12)
  1437→  DOCKYARD_POOL_SIZE      Pool subnet size (default: 24)
  1438→
  1439→Examples:
  1440→  ./dockyard.sh gen-env
  1441→  DOCKYARD_DOCKER_PREFIX=test_ ./dockyard.sh gen-env
  1442→  DOCKYARD_ROOT=/docker2 DOCKYARD_DOCKER_PREFIX=d2_ ./dockyard.sh gen-env
  1443→  ./dockyard.sh gen-env --nocheck
  1444→EOF
  1445→    exit 0
  1446→}
  1447→
  1448→create_usage() {
  1449→    cat <<'EOF'
  1450→Usage: sudo ./dockyard.sh create [OPTIONS]
  1451→
  1452→Create a dockyard instance: download binaries, install config,
  1453→set up systemd service, and start the daemon.
  1454→
  1455→Requires a dockyard.env config file (run gen-env first).
  1456→
  1457→Options:
  1458→  --no-systemd    Skip systemd service installation
  1459→  --no-start      Don't start the daemon after install
  1460→  -h, --help      Show this help
  1461→
  1462→Examples:
  1463→  ./dockyard.sh gen-env && sudo ./dockyard.sh create
  1464→  sudo ./dockyard.sh create --no-systemd --no-start
  1465→  DOCKYARD_ENV=./custom.env sudo -E ./dockyard.sh create
  1466→EOF
  1467→    exit 0
  1468→}
  1469→
  1470→# ── Dispatch ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
  1471→
  1472→COMMAND="${1:-}"
  1473→shift || true
  1474→
  1475→case "$COMMAND" in
  1476→    gen-env)
  1477→        cmd_gen_env "$@"
  1478→        ;;
  1479→    create)
  1480→        if ! try_load_env; then
  1481→            echo "No config found — auto-generating with random networks..."
  1482→            cmd_gen_env
  1483→            load_env
  1484→        fi
  1485→        derive_vars
  1486→        cmd_create "$@"
  1487→        ;;
  1488→    enable)
  1489→        load_env
  1490→        derive_vars
  1491→        cmd_enable
  1492→        ;;
  1493→    disable)
  1494→        load_env
  1495→        derive_vars
  1496→        cmd_disable
  1497→        ;;
  1498→    start)
  1499→        load_env
  1500→        derive_vars
  1501→        cmd_start
  1502→        ;;
  1503→    stop)
  1504→        load_env
  1505→        derive_vars
  1506→        cmd_stop
  1507→        ;;
  1508→    status)
  1509→        load_env
  1510→        derive_vars
  1511→        cmd_status
  1512→        ;;
  1513→    verify)
  1514→        load_env
  1515→        derive_vars
  1516→        cmd_verify
  1517→        ;;
  1518→    destroy)
  1519→        load_env
  1520→        derive_vars
  1521→        cmd_destroy "$@"
  1522→        ;;
  1523→    -h|--help|"")
  1524→        usage
  1525→        ;;
  1526→    *)
  1527→        echo "Unknown command: $COMMAND" >&2
  1528→        usage
  1529→        ;;
  1530→esac
  1531→
  1532→
```

```
   163→setup_ssh_keys() {
   164→  local deploying_user="${SUDO_USER:-}"
   165→
   166→  if [ -z "$deploying_user" ]; then
   167→    warn "No SUDO_USER found — skipping SSH key setup"
   168→    return

   170→
   171→  local source_keys="/home/${deploying_user}/.ssh/authorized_keys"
   172→  local target_dir="${SANDCASTLE_HOME}/.ssh"
   173→  local target_keys="${target_dir}/authorized_keys"
   174→
   175→  if [ ! -f "$source_keys" ]; then
   176→    warn "No SSH keys found for user '${deploying_user}' — skipping SSH key setup"
   177→    return
   178→  fi
   179→
   180→  info "Setting up SSH keys for '${SANDCASTLE_USER}'..."
   181→
   182→  # Create .ssh directory if it doesn't exist
   183→  mkdir -p "$target_dir"
   184→  chmod 700 "$target_dir"
   185→  chown "${SANDCASTLE_USER}:${SANDCASTLE_GROUP}" "$target_dir"
   186→
   187→  # Copy authorized_keys
   188→  cp "$source_keys" "$target_keys"
   189→  chmod 600 "$target_keys"
   190→  chown "${SANDCASTLE_USER}:${SANDCASTLE_GROUP}" "$target_keys"
   191→
   192→  ok "SSH keys configured for '${SANDCASTLE_USER}'"
   193→}

   229→  chown "${SANDCASTLE_USER}:${SANDCASTLE_GROUP}" "$profile"
   230→

   236→  # Add to .bashrc for interactive shells

   259→    ok "Prerequisites installed: ${missing[*]}"

   261→}
```

```
   300→ensure_dirs() {
   301→  mkdir -p "$SANDCASTLE_HOME"/etc
   302→  mkdir -p "$SANDCASTLE_HOME"/bin
   303→  mkdir -p "$SANDCASTLE_HOME"/data/{users,sandboxes,wetty,postgres,snapshots}
   304→  mkdir -p "$SANDCASTLE_HOME"/data/traefik/{dynamic,certs}
   305→  chown "${SANDCASTLE_USER}:${SANDCASTLE_GROUP}" "$SANDCASTLE_HOME"
   306→  # Own top-level data dirs (not -R: per-user subdirs are bind-mounted into
   307→  # Sysbox containers which use a different UID range via /etc/subuid).
   308→  chown "${SANDCASTLE_UID}:${SANDCASTLE_GID}" \
   309→    "$SANDCASTLE_HOME"/data/users \
   310→    "$SANDCASTLE_HOME"/data/sandboxes \
   311→    "$SANDCASTLE_HOME"/data/snapshots \
   312→    "$SANDCASTLE_HOME"/data/wetty
   313→  chown -R "${SANDCASTLE_UID}:${SANDCASTLE_GID}" \
   314→    "$SANDCASTLE_HOME"/data/traefik/dynamic
   315→  # Per-user dirs: own the user-level parent, then chmod 777 the bind-mount
   316→  # targets so Sysbox-mapped root can write to them.
   317→  for d in "$SANDCASTLE_HOME"/data/users/*; do
   318→    [ -d "$d" ] && chown "${SANDCASTLE_UID}:${SANDCASTLE_GID}" "$d"
   319→  done
   320→  for d in "$SANDCASTLE_HOME"/data/users/*/home \
   321→           "$SANDCASTLE_HOME"/data/users/*/data \
   322→           "$SANDCASTLE_HOME"/data/sandboxes/*/vol; do
   323→    [ -d "$d" ] && chmod 777 "$d"
   324→  done
   325→  # Fix home dir permissions inside running sandbox containers so sshd
   326→  # StrictModes is satisfied (rejects 777 home dirs).
   327→  for cid in $($DOCKER ps -q 2>/dev/null); do
   328→    user=$($DOCKER inspect --format '{{range .Config.Env}}{{println .}}{{end}}' "$cid" \
   329→      | grep '^SANDCASTLE_USER=' | cut -d= -f2 || true)
   330→    [ -n "$user" ] && $DOCKER exec "$cid" chmod 755 "/home/$user" 2>/dev/null || true
   331→  done
   332→  usermod -d "$SANDCASTLE_HOME" "$SANDCASTLE_USER" 2>/dev/null || true
   333→  ok "Data directories verified"
   334→}

   336→# ═══ Helpers ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   337→
   338→show_image_info() {
   339→  local repo="$1"
   340→  python3 -c "
   341→import urllib.request, json, sys, re
   342→from datetime import datetime, timezone
   343→repo, arch = '${repo}', '${ARCH}'
   344→try:
   345→    r = urllib.request.urlopen(f'https://ghcr.io/token?scope=repository:thieso2/{repo}:pull')
   346→    token = json.load(r)['token']
   347→    h = {'Authorization': f'Bearer {token}'}
   348→    req = urllib.request.Request(
   349→        f'https://ghcr.io/v2/thieso2/{repo}/manifests/latest',
   350→        headers={**h, 'Accept': 'application/vnd.oci.image.index.v1+json,application/vnd.oci.image.manifest.v1+json'})
   351→    m = json.load(urllib.request.urlopen(req))
   352→    if 'manifests' in m:
   353→        for p in m['manifests']:
   354→            if p.get('platform', {}).get('architecture') == arch:
   355→                req = urllib.request.Request(
   356→                    f'https://ghcr.io/v2/thieso2/{repo}/manifests/{p[\"digest\"]}',
   357→                    headers={**h, 'Accept': 'application/vnd.oci.image.manifest.v1+json'})
   358→                m = json.load(urllib.request.urlopen(req))
   359→                break
   360→    cfg = json.load(urllib.request.urlopen(
   361→        urllib.request.Request(f'https://ghcr.io/v2/thieso2/{repo}/blobs/{m[\"config\"][\"digest\"]}', headers=h)))
   362→    ts = re.sub(r'(\.\d{6})\d+', r'\1', cfg['created']).replace('Z', '+00:00')
   363→    dt = datetime.fromisoformat(ts)
   364→    secs = int((datetime.now(timezone.utc) - dt).total_seconds())
   365→    if secs < 0: secs = 0
   366→    if secs >= 86400: ago = f'{secs // 86400}d ago'
   367→    elif secs >= 3600: ago = f'{secs // 3600}h ago'
   368→    else: ago = f'{max(1, secs // 60)}m ago'
   369→    print(f'  ghcr.io/thieso2/{repo}:latest    built {dt.strftime(\"%Y-%m-%d %H:%M UTC\")} ({ago})')
   370→except Exception:
   371→    print(f'  ghcr.io/thieso2/{repo}:latest    (unable to fetch build info)')
   372→" 2>/dev/null || echo "  ghcr.io/thieso2/${repo}:latest"
   373→}
   374→
   375→pick_private_net() {
   376→  # Pick a random 10.X.0.0/16 not already routed on this host.
   377→  local used_octets
   378→  used_octets=$(
   379→    { ip route 2>/dev/null; ip addr 2>/dev/null; } \
   380→      | grep -oE '10\.[0-9]+\.' \
   381→      | grep -oE '\.[0-9]+\.' \
   382→      | tr -d '.' \
   383→      | sort -un
   384→  )
   385→  for b in $(shuf -i 1-254 2>/dev/null || python3 -c "import random,sys; l=list(range(1,255)); random.shuffle(l); print('\n'.join(map(str,l)))"); do
   386→    if ! echo "$used_octets" | grep -qx "$b"; then
   387→      echo "10.${b}.0.0/16"
   388→      return
   389→    fi
   390→  done
   391→  # Fallback
   392→  echo "10.$(shuf -i 1-254 -n1 2>/dev/null || echo 89).0.0/16"
   393→}
   394→
   395→find_free_subnet() {
   396→  local used
   397→  used=$(
   398→    { ip route 2>/dev/null; ip addr 2>/dev/null; netstat -rn 2>/dev/null; } \
   399→      | grep -oE '(10|172|192)\.[0-9]+\.[0-9]+\.[0-9]+' \
   400→      | sort -un
   401→    $DOCKER network ls -q 2>/dev/null | while read -r nid; do
   402→      $DOCKER network inspect --format '{{range .IPAM.Config}}{{.Subnet}}{{end}}' "$nid" 2>/dev/null
   403→    done | grep -oE '(10|172|192)\.[0-9]+\.[0-9]+\.[0-9]+' \
   404→      | sort -un
   405→  )
   406→  for b in $(shuf -i 16-31); do
   407→    for c in $(shuf -i 1-254 | head -5); do
   408→      if ! echo "$used" | grep -q "^172\.${b}\.${c}\."; then
   409→        echo "172.${b}.${c}.0/24"
   410→        return
   411→      fi
   412→    done
   413→  done
   414→  for b in $(shuf -i 1-254 | head -20); do
   415→    for c in $(shuf -i 1-254 | head -5); do
   416→      if ! echo "$used" | grep -q "^10\.${b}\.${c}\."; then
   417→        echo "10.${b}.${c}.0/24"
   418→        return
   419→      fi
   420→    done
   421→  done
   422→  echo "172.30.99.0/24"
   423→}
   424→
   425→install_mkcert() {
   426→  if command -v mkcert &>/dev/null; then
   427→    return 0
   428→  fi
   429→  info "Installing mkcert..."
   430→  local arch
   431→  arch=$(uname -m)
   432→  local mkcert_arch
   433→  case "$arch" in
   434→    x86_64)  mkcert_arch="amd64" ;;
   435→    aarch64) mkcert_arch="arm64" ;;
   436→    *)       die "Unsupported architecture for mkcert: $arch" ;;
   437→  esac
   438→  curl -fsSL "https://dl.filippo.io/mkcert/latest?for=linux/${mkcert_arch}" \
   439→    -o /usr/local/bin/mkcert
   440→  chmod +x /usr/local/bin/mkcert
   441→  ok "mkcert installed"
   442→}
   443→
   444→# ═══ write_compose ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   445→
   446→# Write the bundled dockyard.sh to /tmp so it can be invoked without wget.
   447→write_dockyard_sh() {
   448→  cat > /tmp/dockyard.sh <<'__DOCKYARD_BUNDLED_EOF__'
   449→@@TEMPLATE:templates/dockyard.sh@@
   450→__DOCKYARD_BUNDLED_EOF__
   451→  chmod +x /tmp/dockyard.sh
   452→}
   453→
   454→write_helper_scripts() {
   455→  cat > "${DOCKYARD_ROOT}/docker-runtime/bin/docker-logs" <<LOGS
   456→#!/bin/bash
   457→exec sudo ${DOCKER} compose -f ${SANDCASTLE_HOME}/docker-compose.yml logs -f "\$@"
   458→LOGS
   459→  chmod +x "${DOCKYARD_ROOT}/docker-runtime/bin/docker-logs"
   460→  wrote "${DOCKYARD_ROOT}/docker-runtime/bin/docker-logs"
   461→}
   462→
   463→write_compose() {
   464→  local DATA_MOUNT="$SANDCASTLE_HOME/data"
   465→
   466→  cat > "$SANDCASTLE_HOME/docker-compose.yml" <<COMPOSE
   467→@@TEMPLATE:docker-compose.yml.template@@
   468→COMPOSE
   469→
   470→  wrote "$SANDCASTLE_HOME/docker-compose.yml"
   471→}
   472→
   473→# ═══ cmd_gen_env ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   474→
   475→cmd_gen_env() {
   476→  local out="${SANDCASTLE_ENV:-./sandcastle.env}"
   477→  [ -f "$out" ] && die "File already exists: $out"
   478→
   479→  local home="${SANDCASTLE_HOME:-/sandcastle}"
   480→  local app_image="${APP_IMAGE:-ghcr.io/thieso2/sandcastle:latest}"
   481→  local sandbox_image="${SANDBOX_IMAGE:-ghcr.io/thieso2/sandcastle-sandbox:latest}"
   482→  local user="${SANDCASTLE_USER:-sandcastle}"
   483→  local group="${SANDCASTLE_GROUP:-$user}"
   484→  local uid="${SANDCASTLE_UID:-220568}"
   485→  local gid="${SANDCASTLE_GID:-220568}"
   486→  local http_port="${SANDCASTLE_HTTP_PORT:-80}"
   487→  local https_port="${SANDCASTLE_HTTPS_PORT:-443}"
   488→  local tls_mode="${SANDCASTLE_TLS_MODE:-selfsigned}"
   489→
   490→  # Auto-detect host IP
   491→  local host="${SANDCASTLE_HOST:-}"
   492→  if [ -z "$host" ]; then
   493→    host=$(hostname -I 2>/dev/null | tr ' ' '\n' | grep -E '^[0-9]+\.' | head -1 || true)
   494→    host="${host:-$(curl -4fsSL --max-time 5 https://ifconfig.me 2>/dev/null || true)}"
   495→    host="${host:-0.0.0.0}"
   496→  fi
   497→
   498→  local admin_email="${SANDCASTLE_ADMIN_EMAIL:-admin@example.com}"
   499→  local admin_user="${SANDCASTLE_ADMIN_USER:-admin}"
```

```
   572→# ═══ cmd_destroy ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   573→
   574→cmd_destroy() {
   575→  local auto_confirm="${1:-false}"
   576→
   577→  require_root
   578→
   579→  # Load installed env for CREATED_USER/CREATED_GROUP
   580→  if [ -f "$SANDCASTLE_HOME/etc/sandcastle.env" ]; then
   581→    # shellcheck source=/dev/null
   582→    source "$SANDCASTLE_HOME/etc/sandcastle.env"
   583→  fi
   584→
   585→  warn "This will remove Sandcastle from $SANDCASTLE_HOME (containers, config, services)"
   586→  if [ "$auto_confirm" != "true" ]; then
   587→    read -rp "Are you sure? (yes to confirm): " CONFIRM
   588→    [ "$CONFIRM" = "yes" ] || die "Aborted"
   589→  fi
   590→
   591→  info "Tearing down Sandcastle..."
   592→  cd /
   593→
   594→  if [ -x "$DOCKER" ]; then
   595→    for container in $($DOCKER ps -a --filter "name=^sc-ts-" --format '{{.Names}}' 2>/dev/null); do
   596→      info "Removing Tailscale sidecar: $container"
   597→      $DOCKER rm -f "$container" 2>/dev/null || true
   598→    done
   599→    for container in $($DOCKER ps -a --filter "runtime=sysbox-runc" --format '{{.Names}}' 2>/dev/null); do
   600→      info "Removing container: $container"
   601→      $DOCKER rm -f "$container" 2>/dev/null || true
   602→    done
   603→    for net in $($DOCKER network ls --filter "name=^sc-ts-net-" --format '{{.Name}}' 2>/dev/null); do
   604→      info "Removing network: $net"
   605→      $DOCKER network rm "$net" 2>/dev/null || true
   606→    done
   607→    if [ -f "$SANDCASTLE_HOME/docker-compose.yml" ]; then
   608→      # Do NOT pass --volumes: postgres data lives in a bind-mount under
   609→      # $SANDCASTLE_HOME/data/postgres which must survive uninstall.
   610→      $DOCKER compose -f "$SANDCASTLE_HOME/docker-compose.yml" down --rmi all --remove-orphans 2>/dev/null || true
   611→    fi
   612→    $DOCKER network rm sandcastle-web 2>/dev/null || true
   613→
   614→    # Remove sandbox image explicitly (not removed by compose down)
   615→    if [ -n "${SANDBOX_IMAGE:-}" ]; then
   616→      info "Removing sandbox image: $SANDBOX_IMAGE"
   617→      $DOCKER rmi "$SANDBOX_IMAGE" 2>/dev/null || true
   618→    fi
   619→  fi
   620→
   621→  # Destroy Dockyard
   622→  DOCKYARD_ENV_FILE="$SANDCASTLE_HOME/etc/dockyard.env"
   623→  if [ -f "$DOCKYARD_ENV_FILE" ] || systemctl cat "${DOCKYARD_DOCKER_PREFIX}docker.service" &>/dev/null; then
   624→    info "Destroying Dockyard..."
   625→    if [ -f "$DOCKYARD_ENV_FILE" ] && write_dockyard_sh 2>/dev/null; then
   626→      echo "y" | DOCKYARD_ENV="$DOCKYARD_ENV_FILE" bash /tmp/dockyard.sh destroy >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
   627→      rm -f /tmp/dockyard.sh
   628→    else
   629→      systemctl stop "${DOCKYARD_DOCKER_PREFIX}docker" 2>/dev/null || true
   630→      systemctl disable "${DOCKYARD_DOCKER_PREFIX}docker" 2>/dev/null || true
   631→      rm -f "/etc/systemd/system/${DOCKYARD_DOCKER_PREFIX}docker.service"
   632→      systemctl daemon-reload 2>/dev/null || true
   633→      ip link delete "${DOCKYARD_DOCKER_PREFIX}docker0" 2>/dev/null || true
   634→    fi
   635→    ok "Dockyard destroyed"
   636→  fi
   637→
   638→  # Remove user and group
   639→  if id "$SANDCASTLE_USER" &>/dev/null; then
   640→    # Do NOT use -r: the user's home ($SANDCASTLE_HOME) contains data that
   641→    # must be preserved across reinstalls. Directories are cleaned up explicitly below.
   642→    userdel "$SANDCASTLE_USER" 2>/dev/null || true
   643→    ok "Removed user '${SANDCASTLE_USER}'"
   644→  fi
   645→  if getent group "$SANDCASTLE_GROUP" &>/dev/null; then
   646→    groupdel "$SANDCASTLE_GROUP" 2>/dev/null || true
   647→    ok "Removed group '${SANDCASTLE_GROUP}'"
   648→  fi
   649→
   650→  # sandcastle-nat.service no longer used (dockyard handles NAT for user-defined networks)
   651→
   652→  # Revert UFW firewall rules
   653→  if command -v ufw &>/dev/null && ufw status 2>/dev/null | grep -q "Status: active"; then
   654→    info "Reverting firewall rules..."
   655→    ufw delete allow "${SANDCASTLE_HTTP_PORT:-80}/tcp" >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
   656→    ufw delete allow "${SANDCASTLE_HTTPS_PORT:-443}/tcp" >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
   657→    ufw delete allow 2201:2299/tcp >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
   658→    ok "Firewall rules reverted"
   659→  fi
   660→
   661→  # Remove sudoers file
   662→  if [ -f /etc/sudoers.d/sandcastle ]; then
   663→    rm -f /etc/sudoers.d/sandcastle
   664→    ok "Removed sudoers file"
   665→  fi
   666→
   667→  # Remove login banner
   668→  if [ -f "/etc/profile.d/sandcastle-banner.sh" ]; then
   669→    rm -f /etc/profile.d/sandcastle-banner.sh
   670→    ok "Removed login banner"
   671→  fi
   672→
   673→  # Remove Sandcastle files — keep user data (data/users, data/sandboxes, data/postgres)
   674→  # Preserve data/traefik/acme.json (Let's Encrypt certs) to survive reinstalls.
   675→  rm -f "$SANDCASTLE_HOME/.env"

   677→  rm -rf "$SANDCASTLE_HOME/etc"

   679→  rm -rf "$SANDCASTLE_HOME/data/traefik/dynamic"
   680→  rm -rf "$SANDCASTLE_HOME/data/traefik/certs"
   681→  rmdir "$SANDCASTLE_HOME/data/traefik" 2>/dev/null || true
   682→  rmdir "$SANDCASTLE_HOME/data" 2>/dev/null || true

   688→  fi
   689→  # Legacy: older installs stored postgres in a Docker named volume 'pgdata'
   690→  if $DOCKER volume inspect pgdata &>/dev/null 2>&1; then
   691→    warn "Legacy Docker volume 'pgdata' still exists — run '$DOCKER volume rm pgdata' to delete it"
   692→  fi
   693→
   694→  ok "Sandcastle destroyed"
   695→}
   696→
   697→# ═══ cmd_install ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   698→
   699→cmd_install() {
   700→  # Parse install-specific options
   701→  local BACKUP_FILE=""
   702→  while [[ $# -gt 0 ]]; do
   703→    case "$1" in
   704→      --from-backup)
   705→        BACKUP_FILE="$2"
   706→        [ -n "$BACKUP_FILE" ] || die "--from-backup requires a backup file path"
   707→        [ -f "$BACKUP_FILE" ] || die "Backup file not found: $BACKUP_FILE"
   708→        shift 2
   709→        ;;
   710→      *)
   711→        die "Unknown option for install: $1 (use --from-backup <file>)"
   712→        ;;
   713→    esac
   714→  done
   715→
   716→  require_root
   717→
   718→  if ! grep -qi ubuntu /etc/os-release 2>/dev/null; then
   719→    warn "This script is tested on Ubuntu 24.04. Other distros may work but are unsupported."
   720→  fi
   721→  if [ "$ARCH" != "amd64" ] && [ "$ARCH" != "arm64" ]; then
   722→    die "Sandcastle requires amd64 or arm64 architecture (got: $ARCH)"
   723→  fi
   724→
   725→  echo ""
   726→  echo -e "${BLUE}═══ Sandcastle Installer ═══${NC}"
   727→  echo ""
   728→  info "Available images (${ARCH}):"
   729→  show_image_info "sandcastle"
   730→  show_image_info "sandcastle-sandbox"
   731→  echo ""
   732→
   733→  # ── Install Dockyard ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   734→
   735→  if [ -S "$DOCKER_SOCK" ]; then
   736→    ok "Dockyard already installed"
   737→    # The docker CLI binary ships with compose built-in. A leftover standalone
   738→    # compose plugin in cli-plugins/ shadows the built-in and may segfault.
   739→    # Remove it so the built-in is used instead.
   740→    local _stale_plugin="${DOCKYARD_ROOT}/docker-runtime/lib/docker/cli-plugins/docker-compose"
   741→    if [ -f "$_stale_plugin" ]; then
   742→      rm -f "$_stale_plugin"
   743→      ok "Removed stale compose plugin (compose is now built into docker CLI)"
   744→    fi
   745→    "$DOCKER" compose version &>/dev/null || die "Docker Compose not available — reinstall Dockyard"
   746→  else
   747→    info "Installing Dockyard (Docker + Sysbox)..."
   748→    write_dockyard_sh
   749→
   750→    mkdir -p "$SANDCASTLE_HOME/etc"
   751→    cat > "$SANDCASTLE_HOME/etc/dockyard.env" <<DYEOF
   752→DOCKYARD_ROOT=${DOCKYARD_ROOT}
   753→DOCKYARD_DOCKER_PREFIX=${DOCKYARD_DOCKER_PREFIX}
   754→DOCKYARD_BRIDGE_CIDR=${DOCKYARD_BRIDGE_CIDR}
   755→DOCKYARD_FIXED_CIDR=${DOCKYARD_FIXED_CIDR}
   756→DOCKYARD_POOL_BASE=${DOCKYARD_POOL_BASE}
   757→DOCKYARD_POOL_SIZE=${DOCKYARD_POOL_SIZE}
   758→DYEOF
   759→    wrote "$SANDCASTLE_HOME/etc/dockyard.env"
   760→
   761→    DOCKYARD_ENV="$SANDCASTLE_HOME/etc/dockyard.env" bash /tmp/dockyard.sh create
   762→    rm -f /tmp/dockyard.sh
   763→
   764→    for i in $(seq 1 30); do
   765→      [ -S "$DOCKER_SOCK" ] && break
   766→      sleep 1
   767→    done
   768→    [ -S "$DOCKER_SOCK" ] || die "Dockyard socket not found at $DOCKER_SOCK after 30s"
   769→    ok "Dockyard installed"
   770→  fi
   771→
   772→  # ── Configure UFW ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   773→
   774→  if command -v ufw &>/dev/null; then
   775→    info "Configuring firewall..."
   776→    ufw --force reset >/dev/null 2>&1
   777→    ufw default deny incoming >/dev/null 2>&1
   778→    ufw default allow outgoing >/dev/null 2>&1
   779→    ufw allow 22/tcp >/dev/null 2>&1
   780→    ufw allow "${SANDCASTLE_HTTP_PORT}/tcp" >/dev/null 2>&1
   781→    ufw allow "${SANDCASTLE_HTTPS_PORT}/tcp" >/dev/null 2>&1
   782→    ufw allow 2201:2299/tcp >/dev/null 2>&1
   783→    ufw allow "${TCP_PORT_MIN}:${TCP_PORT_MAX}/tcp" >/dev/null 2>&1
   784→    ufw --force enable >/dev/null 2>&1
   785→    ok "Firewall configured (22, ${SANDCASTLE_HTTP_PORT}, ${SANDCASTLE_HTTPS_PORT}, 2201-2299, ${TCP_PORT_MIN}-${TCP_PORT_MAX})"
   786→  else
   787→    warn "UFW not found — skipping firewall setup"
   788→  fi
   789→
   790→  # ── Create system user/group ──────────────────────────────────────────────
   791→
   792→  CREATED_GROUP=false
   793→  CREATED_USER=false
   794→
   795→  if getent group "$SANDCASTLE_GROUP" &>/dev/null; then
   796→    ok "Group '${SANDCASTLE_GROUP}' already exists"
   797→  else
   798→    groupadd --system --gid "$SANDCASTLE_GID" "$SANDCASTLE_GROUP"
   799→    CREATED_GROUP=true
```

```
   871→      # shellcheck source=/dev/null
   872→      source "$POSTGRES_SECRETS_FILE"
   873→      info "Using database password from backup"
   874→
   875→      # Load AR encryption keys from backup
   876→      RAILS_SECRETS_FILE="$SANDCASTLE_HOME/data/rails/.secrets"
   877→      mkdir -p "$SANDCASTLE_HOME/data/rails"
   878→      cp "$_bk_work/sandcastle-backup/secrets/rails.secrets" "$RAILS_SECRETS_FILE"
   879→      chmod 600 "$RAILS_SECRETS_FILE"
   880→      # shellcheck source=/dev/null

   882→      info "Using AR encryption keys from backup"
   883→

   888→
   889→      # Preserve DB password across reinstalls (user data)

   891→      if [ -f "$POSTGRES_SECRETS_FILE" ]; then

   894→        info "Reusing existing database password"
   895→      else
   896→        DB_PASSWORD=$(openssl rand -hex 32)
   897→        mkdir -p "$SANDCASTLE_HOME/data/postgres"
   898→        echo "DB_PASSWORD=$DB_PASSWORD" > "$POSTGRES_SECRETS_FILE"
   899→        chmod 600 "$POSTGRES_SECRETS_FILE"
   900→        wrote "$POSTGRES_SECRETS_FILE"
   901→      fi
   902→
   903→      # Preserve AR encryption keys across reinstalls — required to decrypt
   904→      # encrypted settings (SMTP password, OAuth secrets) stored in the database.
   905→      # Each install gets unique keys; losing them makes encrypted values unrecoverable.
   906→      RAILS_SECRETS_FILE="$SANDCASTLE_HOME/data/rails/.secrets"
   907→      if [ -f "$RAILS_SECRETS_FILE" ]; then
   908→        # shellcheck source=/dev/null
   909→        source "$RAILS_SECRETS_FILE"
   910→        info "Reusing existing AR encryption keys"
   911→      else

   915→        AR_ENCRYPTION_KEY_DERIVATION_SALT=$(openssl rand -hex 16)

   918→AR_ENCRYPTION_DETERMINISTIC_KEY=$AR_ENCRYPTION_DETERMINISTIC_KEY
   919→AR_ENCRYPTION_KEY_DERIVATION_SALT=$AR_ENCRYPTION_KEY_DERIVATION_SALT
   920→SECRETS
   921→        chmod 600 "$RAILS_SECRETS_FILE"
   922→        wrote "$RAILS_SECRETS_FILE"
   923→        warn "AR encryption keys generated. Back them up — losing them makes encrypted settings unrecoverable."
   924→      fi

   960→

   963→
   964→  # Backfill vars that may be missing in older .env files

   988→AR_ENCRYPTION_DETERMINISTIC_KEY=$AR_ENCRYPTION_DETERMINISTIC_KEY

  1002→  # Backfill OAuth vars from sandcastle.env into .env
  1003→  grep -q '^GITHUB_CLIENT_ID=' "$SANDCASTLE_HOME/.env" 2>/dev/null || \

  1011→
  1012→  # ── Write installed sandcastle.env ────────────────────────────────────────

  1014→  cat > "$SANDCASTLE_HOME/etc/sandcastle.env" <<EOF
  1015→# Sandcastle installed configuration — generated $(date -Iseconds)

  1017→APP_IMAGE="${APP_IMAGE}"
  1018→SANDBOX_IMAGE="${SANDBOX_IMAGE}"

  1020→SANDCASTLE_GROUP="${SANDCASTLE_GROUP}"

  1048→  chmod 600 "$SANDCASTLE_HOME/etc/sandcastle.env"

  1050→
  1051→  # ── Traefik config ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  1052→
  1053→  TRAEFIK_DIR="$SANDCASTLE_HOME/data/traefik"
  1054→
  1055→  if [ "$SANDCASTLE_TLS_MODE" = "selfsigned" ]; then
  1056→    if [ ! -f "$TRAEFIK_DIR/certs/cert.pem" ]; then
  1057→      info "Generating self-signed certificate..."
  1058→      openssl req -x509 -newkey rsa:4096 -sha256 -days 3650 -nodes \
  1059→        -keyout "$TRAEFIK_DIR/certs/key.pem" -out "$TRAEFIK_DIR/certs/cert.pem" \
  1060→        -subj "/CN=$SANDCASTLE_HOST" \
  1061→        -addext "subjectAltName=IP:$SANDCASTLE_HOST" 2>/dev/null
  1062→      ok "Self-signed certificate generated"
  1063→    fi
  1064→
  1065→    cat > "$TRAEFIK_DIR/traefik.yml" <<'TEOF'
  1066→entryPoints:
  1067→  web:
  1068→    address: ":80"
  1069→    http:
  1070→      redirections:
  1071→        entryPoint:
  1072→          to: websecure
  1073→          scheme: https
  1074→  websecure:
  1075→    address: ":443"
  1076→
  1077→providers:
  1078→  file:
  1079→    directory: /data/dynamic
  1080→    watch: true
  1081→
  1082→tls:
  1083→  certificates:
  1084→    - certFile: /data/certs/cert.pem
  1085→      keyFile: /data/certs/key.pem
  1086→
  1087→log:
  1088→  level: INFO
  1089→
  1090→api:
  1091→  dashboard: false
  1092→TEOF
  1093→
  1094→  elif [ "$SANDCASTLE_TLS_MODE" = "mkcert" ]; then
  1095→    install_mkcert
  1096→    local caroot="$SANDCASTLE_HOME/data/traefik/certs"
  1097→    CAROOT="$caroot" mkcert -install 2>/dev/null || true
  1098→    if [ ! -f "$TRAEFIK_DIR/certs/cert.pem" ]; then
  1099→      info "Generating mkcert certificate for $SANDCASTLE_HOST..."
  1100→      CAROOT="$caroot" mkcert \
  1101→        -cert-file "$TRAEFIK_DIR/certs/cert.pem" \
  1102→        -key-file  "$TRAEFIK_DIR/certs/key.pem" \
  1103→        "$SANDCASTLE_HOST" "*.${SANDCASTLE_HOST}" localhost 127.0.0.1 ::1
  1104→      ok "mkcert certificate generated"
  1105→    fi
  1106→    cp "$caroot/rootCA.pem" "$TRAEFIK_DIR/certs/rootCA.pem" 2>/dev/null || true
  1107→    info "mkcert CA root: $caroot/rootCA.pem"
  1108→    info "Install on client machines to trust this server's certificate:"
  1109→    info "  macOS: sudo security add-trusted-cert -d -r trustRoot -k /Library/Keychains/System.keychain rootCA.pem"
  1110→    info "  Linux: sudo cp rootCA.pem /usr/local/share/ca-certificates/sandcastle.crt && sudo update-ca-certificates"
  1111→
  1112→    cat > "$TRAEFIK_DIR/traefik.yml" <<'TEOF'
  1113→entryPoints:
  1114→  web:
  1115→    address: ":80"
  1116→    http:
  1117→      redirections:
  1118→        entryPoint:
  1119→          to: websecure
  1120→          scheme: https
  1121→  websecure:
  1122→    address: ":443"
  1123→
  1124→providers:
  1125→  file:
  1126→    directory: /data/dynamic
  1127→    watch: true
  1128→
  1129→tls:
  1130→  certificates:
  1131→    - certFile: /data/certs/cert.pem
  1132→      keyFile: /data/certs/key.pem
  1133→
  1134→log:
  1135→  level: INFO
  1136→
  1137→api:
  1138→  dashboard: false
  1139→TEOF
  1140→
  1141→  else
  1142→    cat > "$TRAEFIK_DIR/traefik.yml" <<TEOF
  1143→entryPoints:
  1144→  web:
  1145→    address: ":80"
  1146→    http:
  1147→      redirections:
  1148→        entryPoint:
  1149→          to: websecure
  1150→          scheme: https
  1151→  websecure:
  1152→    address: ":443"
  1153→
  1154→certificatesResolvers:
  1155→  letsencrypt:
  1156→    acme:
  1157→      email: "${ACME_EMAIL}"
  1158→      storage: /data/acme.json
  1159→      httpChallenge:
  1160→        entryPoint: web
  1161→
  1162→providers:
  1163→  file:
  1164→    directory: /data/dynamic
  1165→    watch: true
  1166→
  1167→log:
  1168→  level: INFO
  1169→
  1170→api:
  1171→  dashboard: false
  1172→TEOF
  1173→  fi

  1175→
  1176→  if [ ! -f "$TRAEFIK_DIR/acme.json" ]; then
  1177→    touch "$TRAEFIK_DIR/acme.json"

  1179→  fi
  1180→

  1191→  services:

  1195→          - url: "http://sandcastle-web:80"
  1196→TEOF
  1197→  else

  1199→http:

  1209→    rails:

  1211→        servers:
  1212→          - url: "http://sandcastle-web:80"
  1213→TEOF
  1214→  fi

  1216→
  1217→  chown "${SANDCASTLE_UID}:${SANDCASTLE_GID}" "$TRAEFIK_DIR/traefik.yml"

  1219→
  1220→  # ── Docker network ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  1221→
  1222→  # ── Create sandcastle-web network (allocated from dockyard pool) ────────────
  1223→  # Let dockyard allocate the subnet from its address pool (DOCKYARD_POOL_BASE)
  1224→  # so dockyard's own iptables rules handle NAT/forwarding automatically.
  1225→  # No separate sandcastle-nat service needed.
  1226→  if $DOCKER network inspect sandcastle-web &>/dev/null; then
  1227→    ok "sandcastle-web network exists"
  1228→  else
  1229→    $DOCKER network create sandcastle-web >/dev/null
  1230→    ok "sandcastle-web network created"
  1231→  fi
  1232→
  1233→  # ── Pull images ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  1238→  $DOCKER pull traefik:v3.6 &
  1239→  wait
  1240→  ok "Images pulled"
  1241→
  1242→  # ── Write PostgreSQL init script ──────────────────────────────────────────
  1243→
  1244→  mkdir -p "$SANDCASTLE_HOME/etc/postgres"
  1245→  cat > "$REDACTED.sh" <<'INITDB'
  1246→@@TEMPLATE:/docker/postgres/init-databases.sh@@
  1247→INITDB
  1248→  chmod +x "$REDACTED.sh"
  1249→  wrote "$REDACTED.sh"
  1250→
  1251→  write_compose
  1252→
  1253→  write_helper_scripts
  1254→
  1255→  # ── Install prerequisites and sandcastle-admin ────────────────────────────
  1256→
  1257→  install_prerequisites
  1258→  write_admin_script
  1259→
  1260→  # ── Start services ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  1261→
  1262→  cd "$SANDCASTLE_HOME"
  1263→
  1264→  if [ "$FRESH_INSTALL" = true ] && [ -n "$BACKUP_FILE" ]; then
  1265→    # ── Restore from backup ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
  1266→    # Start postgres first, restore data, then bring up everything.
  1267→
  1268→    info "Starting PostgreSQL for restore..."
  1269→    $DOCKER compose up -d postgres
  1270→    local _i
  1271→    for _i in $(seq 1 30); do
  1272→      $DOCKER compose exec -T postgres \
  1273→        pg_isready -U sandcastle -d sandcastle_production &>/dev/null && break
  1274→      sleep 2
  1275→    done
  1276→    ok "PostgreSQL ready"
  1277→
  1278→    # Extract and restore from the backup (db + data + images).
  1279→    # Use sandcastle-admin restore for the actual restore logic.
  1280→    info "Restoring from backup: $BACKUP_FILE"
  1281→    SANDCASTLE_HOME="$SANDCASTLE_HOME" \
  1282→      "$REDACTED" restore "$BACKUP_FILE" \
  1283→      --yes --skip-images 2>/dev/null \
  1284→      || die "Restore failed — check the backup file and try again"
  1285→
  1286→    # Load snapshot images (docker save/load requires the daemon to be running)
  1287→    # The restore command skipped them above; load manually now if present.
  1288→    info "Starting Sandcastle..."
  1289→    $DOCKER compose up -d
  1290→
  1291→    # Load snapshot images now that the Dockyard daemon is fully up
  1292→    local _tmp_bk
  1293→    _tmp_bk=$(mktemp -d)
  1294→    tar --use-compress-program=zstd -xf "$BACKUP_FILE" -C "$_tmp_bk" \
  1295→      sandcastle-backup/images 2>/dev/null || true
  1296→    if [ -d "$_tmp_bk/sandcastle-backup/images" ]; then
  1297→      info "Loading snapshot Docker images..."
  1298→      local _img
  1299→      for _img in "$_tmp_bk/sandcastle-backup/images/"*.tar; do
```

```
  1325→        -e SANDCASTLE_ADMIN_PASSWORD="${SANDCASTLE_ADMIN_PASSWORD}" \
  1326→        -e SANDCASTLE_ADMIN_SSH_KEY="${SANDCASTLE_ADMIN_SSH_KEY:-}" \
  1327→        web ./bin/rails db:seed
  1328→    fi
  1329→  fi
  1330→
  1331→  # ── Done ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  1332→
  1333→  # Build base URL with port suffix only when non-standard
  1334→  local port_suffix=""
  1335→  [ "$SANDCASTLE_HTTPS_PORT" != "443" ] && port_suffix=":${SANDCASTLE_HTTPS_PORT}"
  1336→  local base_url="https://${SANDCASTLE_HOST}${port_suffix}"
  1337→
  1338→  echo ""
  1339→  echo -e "${GREEN}═══════════════════════════════════════════════════${NC}"
  1340→  echo -e "${GREEN}  Sandcastle is running!${NC}"
  1341→  echo -e "${GREEN}═══════════════════════════════════════════════════${NC}"
  1342→  echo ""
  1343→
  1344→  if [ "$SANDCASTLE_TLS_MODE" = "selfsigned" ]; then
  1345→    echo -e "  Dashboard:  ${BLUE}${base_url}${NC} (self-signed cert)"
  1346→  elif [ "$SANDCASTLE_TLS_MODE" = "mkcert" ]; then
  1347→    echo -e "  Dashboard:  ${BLUE}${base_url}${NC} (mkcert cert — install rootCA.pem on clients)"
  1348→  else
  1349→    echo -e "  Dashboard:  ${BLUE}${base_url}${NC}"
  1350→  fi
  1351→
  1352→  if [ "$FRESH_INSTALL" = true ] && [ -n "$BACKUP_FILE" ]; then
  1353→    echo ""
  1354→    echo -e "  ${GREEN}Restored from backup:${NC} $BACKUP_FILE"
  1355→    echo -e "  Log in with your existing credentials."
  1356→  elif [ "$FRESH_INSTALL" = true ]; then
  1357→    echo ""
  1358→    echo -e "  Admin login:"
  1359→    echo -e "    Email:     ${YELLOW}${SANDCASTLE_ADMIN_EMAIL}${NC}"
  1360→    echo ""
  1361→    echo -e "  Tailscale:   ${BLUE}${base_url}/tailscale${NC}"
  1362→  fi
  1363→
  1364→  echo ""
  1365→  echo -e "  Home:       $SANDCASTLE_HOME"
  1366→  echo -e "  Config:     $SANDCASTLE_HOME/etc/sandcastle.env"
  1367→  echo -e "  Docker:     $DOCKER"
  1368→  echo -e "  Logs:       ${DOCKYARD_ROOT}/docker-runtime/bin/docker-logs"
  1369→
  1370→  print_written_files
  1371→  echo ""
  1372→}
  1373→
  1374→# ═══ cmd_update ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  1375→
  1376→cmd_update() {
  1377→  require_root
  1378→
  1379→  [ ! -f "$SANDCASTLE_HOME/.env" ] && die "No existing install found at $SANDCASTLE_HOME — run 'install' first"
  1380→
  1381→  # shellcheck source=/dev/null
  1382→  source "$SANDCASTLE_HOME/.env"
  1383→
  1384→  # Backfill vars that may be missing in older .env files
  1385→  grep -q '^DOCKYARD_POOL_BASE=' "$SANDCASTLE_HOME/.env" 2>/dev/null || \
  1386→    echo "DOCKYARD_POOL_BASE=${DOCKYARD_POOL_BASE}" >> "$SANDCASTLE_HOME/.env"
  1387→  if ! grep -q '^AR_ENCRYPTION_PRIMARY_KEY=' "$SANDCASTLE_HOME/.env" 2>/dev/null; then
  1388→    RAILS_SECRETS_FILE="$SANDCASTLE_HOME/data/rails/.secrets"
  1389→    if [ -f "$RAILS_SECRETS_FILE" ]; then
  1390→      # shellcheck source=/dev/null
  1391→      source "$RAILS_SECRETS_FILE"
  1392→    else
  1393→      mkdir -p "$SANDCASTLE_HOME/data/rails"
  1394→      AR_ENCRYPTION_PRIMARY_KEY=$(openssl rand -hex 16)
  1395→      AR_ENCRYPTION_DETERMINISTIC_KEY=$(openssl rand -hex 16)
  1396→      AR_ENCRYPTION_KEY_DERIVATION_SALT=$(openssl rand -hex 16)
  1397→      cat > "$RAILS_SECRETS_FILE" <<SECRETS
  1398→AR_ENCRYPTION_PRIMARY_KEY=$AR_ENCRYPTION_PRIMARY_KEY
  1399→AR_ENCRYPTION_DETERMINISTIC_KEY=$AR_ENCRYPTION_DETERMINISTIC_KEY
  1400→AR_ENCRYPTION_KEY_DERIVATION_SALT=$AR_ENCRYPTION_KEY_DERIVATION_SALT
  1401→SECRETS
  1402→      chmod 600 "$RAILS_SECRETS_FILE"
  1403→      wrote "$RAILS_SECRETS_FILE"
  1404→      warn "AR encryption keys generated. Back them up — losing them makes encrypted settings unrecoverable."
  1405→    fi
  1406→    cat >> "$SANDCASTLE_HOME/.env" <<ARKEYS
  1407→AR_ENCRYPTION_PRIMARY_KEY=$AR_ENCRYPTION_PRIMARY_KEY
  1408→AR_ENCRYPTION_DETERMINISTIC_KEY=$AR_ENCRYPTION_DETERMINISTIC_KEY
  1409→AR_ENCRYPTION_KEY_DERIVATION_SALT=$AR_ENCRYPTION_KEY_DERIVATION_SALT
  1410→ARKEYS
  1411→    ok "AR encryption keys backfilled into .env"
  1412→  fi
  1413→  grep -q '^GITHUB_CLIENT_ID=' "$SANDCASTLE_HOME/.env" 2>/dev/null || \
  1414→    echo "GITHUB_CLIENT_ID=${GITHUB_CLIENT_ID:-}" >> "$SANDCASTLE_HOME/.env"
  1415→  grep -q '^GITHUB_CLIENT_SECRET=' "$SANDCASTLE_HOME/.env" 2>/dev/null || \
  1416→    echo "GITHUB_CLIENT_SECRET=${GITHUB_CLIENT_SECRET:-}" >> "$SANDCASTLE_HOME/.env"
  1417→  grep -q '^GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID=' "$SANDCASTLE_HOME/.env" 2>/dev/null || \
  1418→    echo "GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID=${GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID:-}" >> "$SANDCASTLE_HOME/.env"
  1419→  grep -q '^GOOGLE_CLIENT_SECRET=' "$SANDCASTLE_HOME/.env" 2>/dev/null || \
  1420→    echo "GOOGLE_CLIENT_SECRET=${GOOGLE_CLIENT_SECRET:-}" >> "$SANDCASTLE_HOME/.env"
  1421→
  1422→  echo ""
  1423→  echo -e "${BLUE}═══ Sandcastle Update ═══${NC}"
  1424→  echo ""
  1425→  info "Available images (${ARCH}):"
  1426→  show_image_info "sandcastle"
  1427→  show_image_info "sandcastle-sandbox"
  1428→  echo ""
  1429→
  1430→  ensure_dirs
  1431→
  1432→  info "Pulling images..."
  1433→  $DOCKER pull "$APP_IMAGE" &
  1434→  $DOCKER pull "$SANDBOX_IMAGE" &
  1435→  wait
  1436→  ok "Images pulled"
  1437→
  1438→  write_compose
  1439→  write_helper_scripts
  1440→
  1441→  info "Restarting services..."
  1442→  cd "$SANDCASTLE_HOME"
  1443→  $DOCKER compose up -d
  1444→  ok "Services restarted"
  1445→
  1446→  echo ""
  1447→  echo -e "${GREEN}  Sandcastle updated!${NC}"
  1448→
  1449→  print_written_files
  1450→  echo ""
  1451→}
  1452→
  1453→# ═══ Dispatch ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  1454→
  1455→case "$COMMAND" in
  1456→  gen-env)    cmd_gen_env ;;
  1457→  install)    load_env; derive_vars; cmd_install "$@" ;;
  1458→  update)     load_env; derive_vars; cmd_update ;;
  1459→  reset)      load_env; derive_vars; cmd_destroy true; cmd_install "$@" ;;
  1460→  uninstall)  load_env; derive_vars; cmd_destroy ;;
  1461→esac
  1462→
```

```
     1→#!/usr/bin/env bash
     2→# Build installer.sh from installer.sh.in template
     3→set -euo pipefail
     4→
     5→SCRIPT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
     6→REPO_ROOT="$(cd "$SCRIPT_DIR/.." && pwd)"
     7→TEMPLATES_DIR="$SCRIPT_DIR/templates"
     8→TEMPLATE_FILE="$SCRIPT_DIR/installer.sh.in"
     9→OUTPUT_FILE="$REPO_ROOT/installer.sh"
    10→
    11→# Colors for output
    12→RED='\033[0;31m'
    13→GREEN='\033[0;32m'
    14→YELLOW='\033[1;33m'
    15→NC='\033[0m' # No Color
    16→
    17→info() { echo -e "${GREEN}→${NC} $*" >&2; }
    18→warn() { echo -e "${YELLOW}⚠${NC} $*" >&2; }
    19→error() { echo -e "${RED}✗${NC} $*" >&2; }
    20→ok() { echo -e "${GREEN}✓${NC} $*" >&2; }
    21→
    22→if [ ! -f "$TEMPLATE_FILE" ]; then
    23→  error "Template file not found: $TEMPLATE_FILE"
    24→  error "Run: cp installer.sh installer/installer.sh.in"
    25→  error "Then replace heredocs with @@TEMPLATE:filename@@ markers"
    26→  exit 1
    27→fi
    28→
    29→info "Building installer.sh from template..."
    30→
    31→# Create temporary output file
    32→TEMP_OUTPUT=$(mktemp)
    33→
    34→# Process the template line by line
    35→while IFS= read -r line; do
    36→  # Check for template markers: @@TEMPLATE:filename@@
    37→  if [[ "$line" =~ @@TEMPLATE:([^@]+)@@ ]]; then
    38→    template_file="${BASH_REMATCH[1]}"
    39→
    40→    # Determine the full path to the template
    41→    if [[ "$template_file" == /* ]]; then
    42→      # Absolute path from repo root
    43→      full_path="$REPO_ROOT/${template_file#/}"
    44→    elif [[ "$template_file" == templates/* ]]; then
    45→      # Relative to installer/templates/
    46→      full_path="$TEMPLATES_DIR/${template_file#templates/}"
    47→    else
    48→      # Assume it's in templates/
    49→      full_path="$TEMPLATES_DIR/$template_file"
    50→    fi
    51→
    52→    if [ ! -f "$full_path" ]; then
    53→      error "Template file not found: $full_path"
    54→      rm -f "$TEMP_OUTPUT"
    55→      exit 1
    56→    fi
    57→
    58→    info "  Injecting: $template_file"
    59→    cat "$full_path"
    60→  else
    61→    # Regular line, output as-is
    62→    echo "$line"
    63→  fi
    64→done < "$TEMPLATE_FILE" > "$TEMP_OUTPUT"
    65→
    66→# Move to final location
    67→mv "$TEMP_OUTPUT" "$OUTPUT_FILE"
    68→chmod +x "$OUTPUT_FILE"
    69→
    70→ok "Built: $OUTPUT_FILE"
    71→echo ""
    72→info "Verify the output and test before committing!"
    73→
```

> AGENT

Now I have everything. Here's my plan for the migration:

**Key changes needed:**

1. **Replace `installer/templates/dockyard.sh`** with the new dockyard.sh
2. **Update `derive_vars()`** — new paths: `DOCKER_SOCK` = `$DOCKYARD_ROOT/run/docker.sock`, `DOCKER` = `$DOCKYARD_ROOT/bin/docker`
3. **Update `cmd_install()`** — dockyard `create` now expects `DOCKYARD_ROOT` separate from `SANDCASTLE_HOME`. Need to use `DOCKYARD_ROOT=$SANDCASTLE_HOME/dockyard` or keep it at `$SANDCASTLE_HOME` and handle the new directory layout
4. **Update `cmd_destroy()`** — new dockyard uses `destroy --yes` instead of piping `echo "y"`
5. **Update `setup_bashrc_path()`** — new bin path
6. **Update `write_helper_scripts()`** — new docker path
7. **Update docker-compose template** — `DOCKER_SOCK` path changes
8. **Handle migration from old layout** — old `docker-runtime/bin/docker` → new `bin/docker`, old `docker.sock` → new `run/docker.sock`

The critical decision: where to put DOCKYARD_ROOT. Currently `DOCKYARD_ROOT=/sandcastle` and the new dockyard creates `$ROOT/bin/`, `$ROOT/etc/`, `$ROOT/lib/`, `$ROOT/run/`. This would conflict with Sandcastle's own `etc/`, `data/` etc. 

I think the cleanest approach is: `DOCKYARD_ROOT=$SANDCASTLE_HOME/dockyard`. This keeps dockyard isolated. Let me implement this.

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle/installer/installer.sh.in",
  "old_string": "  # Destroy Dockyard\n  DOCKYARD_ENV_FILE=\"$SANDCASTLE_HOME/etc/dockyard.env\"\n  if [ -f \"$DOCKYARD_ENV_FILE\" ] || systemctl cat \"${DOCKYARD_DOCKER_PREFIX}docker.service\" &>/dev/null; then\n    info \"Destroying Dockyard...\"\n    if [ -f \"$DOCKYARD_ENV_FILE\" ] && write_dockyard_sh 2>/dev/null; then\n      echo \"y\" | DOCKYARD_ENV=\"$DOCKYARD_ENV_FILE\" bash /tmp/dockyard.sh destroy >/dev/null 2>&1 || true\n      rm -f /tmp/dockyard.sh\n    else\n      systemctl stop \"${DOCKYARD_DOCKER_PREFIX}docker\" 2>/dev/null || true\n      systemctl disable \"${DOCKYARD_DOCKER_PREFIX}docker\" 2>/dev/null || true\n      rm -f \"/etc/systemd/system/${DOCKYARD_DOCKER_PREFIX}docker.service\"\n      systemctl daemon-reload 2>/dev/null || true\n      ip link delete \"${DOCKYARD_DOCKER_PREFIX}docker0\" 2>/dev/null || true\n    fi\n    ok \"Dockyard destroyed\"\n  fi",
  "new_string": "  # Destroy Dockyard — handle both new layout ($DOCKYARD_ROOT/etc/dockyard.env)\n  # and legacy layout ($SANDCASTLE_HOME/etc/dockyard.env with docker-runtime/)\n  DOCKYARD_ENV_FILE=\"\"\n  if [ -f \"$DOCKYARD_ROOT/etc/dockyard.env\" ]; then\n    DOCKYARD_ENV_FILE=\"$DOCKYARD_ROOT/etc/dockyard.env\"\n  elif [ -f \"$SANDCASTLE_HOME/etc/dockyard.env\" ]; then\n    DOCKYARD_ENV_FILE=\"$SANDCASTLE_HOME/etc/dockyard.env\"\n  fi\n\n  if [ -n \"$DOCKYARD_ENV_FILE\" ] || systemctl cat \"${DOCKYARD_DOCKER_PREFIX}docker.service\" &>/dev/null; then\n    info \"Destroying Dockyard...\"\n    if [ -n \"$DOCKYARD_ENV_FILE\" ] && write_dockyard_sh 2>/dev/null; then\n      DOCKYARD_ENV=\"$DOCKYARD_ENV_FILE\" bash /tmp/dockyard.sh destroy --yes 2>&1 || true\n      rm -f /tmp/dockyard.sh\n    else\n      systemctl stop \"${DOCKYARD_DOCKER_PREFIX}docker\" 2>/dev/null || true\n      systemctl disable \"${DOCKYARD_DOCKER_PREFIX}docker\" 2>/dev/null || true\n      rm -f \"/etc/systemd/system/${DOCKYARD_DOCKER_PREFIX}docker.service\"\n      systemctl daemon-reload 2>/dev/null || true\n      ip link delete \"${DOCKYARD_DOCKER_PREFIX}docker0\" 2>/dev/null || true\n    fi\n    # Clean up legacy sc_sysbox.service (old two-service layout)\n    if systemctl cat \"${DOCKYARD_DOCKER_PREFIX}sysbox.service\" &>/dev/null 2>&1; then\n      systemctl stop \"${DOCKYARD_DOCKER_PREFIX}sysbox\" 2>/dev/null || true\n      systemctl disable \"${DOCKYARD_DOCKER_PREFIX}sysbox\" 2>/dev/null || true\n      rm -f \"/etc/systemd/system/${DOCKYARD_DOCKER_PREFIX}sysbox.service\"\n      systemctl daemon-reload 2>/dev/null || true\n    fi\n    # Clean up legacy docker-runtime directory (pre-dockyard layout)\n    rm -rf \"$REDACTED\"\n    # Clean up legacy socket and docker data at old paths\n    rm -f \"$SANDCASTLE_HOME/docker.sock\"\n    rm -rf \"$SANDCASTLE_HOME/docker\"\n    rm -rf \"$SANDCASTLE_HOME/sysbox\"\n    ok \"Dockyard destroyed\"\n  fi"
}
```

```json
  "old_string": "  if [ -S \"$DOCKER_SOCK\" ]; then\n    ok \"Dockyard already installed\"\n    # The docker CLI binary ships with compose built-in. A leftover standalone\n    # compose plugin in cli-plugins/ shadows the built-in and may segfault.\n    # Remove it so the built-in is used instead.\n    local _stale_plugin=\"${DOCKYARD_ROOT}/docker-runtime/lib/docker/cli-plugins/docker-compose\"\n    if [ -f \"$_stale_plugin\" ]; then\n      rm -f \"$_stale_plugin\"\n      ok \"Removed stale compose plugin (compose is now built into docker CLI)\"\n    fi\n    \"$DOCKER\" compose version &>/dev/null || die \"Docker Compose not available — reinstall Dockyard\"\n  else\n    info \"Installing Dockyard (Docker + Sysbox)...\"\n    write_dockyard_sh\n\n    mkdir -p \"$SANDCASTLE_HOME/etc\"\n    cat > \"$SANDCASTLE_HOME/etc/dockyard.env\" <<DYEOF\nDOCKYARD_ROOT=${DOCKYARD_ROOT}\nDOCKYARD_DOCKER_PREFIX=${DOCKYARD_DOCKER_PREFIX}\nDOCKYARD_BRIDGE_CIDR=${DOCKYARD_BRIDGE_CIDR}\nDOCKYARD_FIXED_CIDR=${DOCKYARD_FIXED_CIDR}\nDOCKYARD_POOL_BASE=${DOCKYARD_POOL_BASE}\nDOCKYARD_POOL_SIZE=${DOCKYARD_POOL_SIZE}\nDYEOF\n    wrote \"$SANDCASTLE_HOME/etc/dockyard.env\"\n\n    DOCKYARD_ENV=\"$SANDCASTLE_HOME/etc/dockyard.env\" bash /tmp/dockyard.sh create\n    rm -f /tmp/dockyard.sh\n\n    for i in $(seq 1 30); do\n      [ -S \"$DOCKER_SOCK\" ] && break\n      sleep 1\n    done\n    [ -S \"$DOCKER_SOCK\" ] || die \"Dockyard socket not found at $DOCKER_SOCK after 30s\"\n    ok \"Dockyard installed\"\n  fi",
  "new_string": "  if [ -S \"$DOCKER_SOCK\" ]; then\n    ok \"Dockyard already installed\"\n    \"$DOCKER\" compose version &>/dev/null || die \"Docker Compose not available — reinstall Dockyard\"\n  else\n    info \"Installing Dockyard (Docker + Sysbox)...\"\n    write_dockyard_sh\n\n    mkdir -p \"$SANDCASTLE_HOME/etc\"\n    local _dy_env=\"$SANDCASTLE_HOME/etc/dockyard.env\"\n    cat > \"$_dy_env\" <<DYEOF\nDOCKYARD_ROOT=${DOCKYARD_ROOT}\nDOCKYARD_DOCKER_PREFIX=${DOCKYARD_DOCKER_PREFIX}\nDOCKYARD_BRIDGE_CIDR=${DOCKYARD_BRIDGE_CIDR}\nDOCKYARD_FIXED_CIDR=${DOCKYARD_FIXED_CIDR}\nDOCKYARD_POOL_BASE=${DOCKYARD_POOL_BASE}\nDOCKYARD_POOL_SIZE=${DOCKYARD_POOL_SIZE}\nDYEOF\n    wrote \"$_dy_env\"\n\n    DOCKYARD_ENV=\"$_dy_env\" bash /tmp/dockyard.sh create\n    rm -f /tmp/dockyard.sh\n\n    for i in $(seq 1 30); do\n      [ -S \"$DOCKER_SOCK\" ] && break\n      sleep 1\n    done\n    [ -S \"$DOCKER_SOCK\" ] || die \"Dockyard socket not found at $DOCKER_SOCK after 30s\"\n    ok \"Dockyard installed\"\n  fi"
}
```

```json
  "new_string": "  # Add sandcastle user to dockyard docker group for socket access\n  DOCKER_GROUP=\"${DOCKYARD_DOCKER_PREFIX}docker\"\n  if getent group \"$DOCKER_GROUP\" &>/dev/null; then\n    usermod -aG \"$DOCKER_GROUP\" \"$SANDCASTLE_USER\"\n    ok \"Added '${SANDCASTLE_USER}' to group '${DOCKER_GROUP}'\"\n  else\n    # Fallback: detect from socket\n    DOCKER_GROUP=$(stat -c '%G' \"$DOCKER_SOCK\" 2>/dev/null || echo \"docker\")\n    if getent group \"$DOCKER_GROUP\" &>/dev/null; then\n      usermod -aG \"$DOCKER_GROUP\" \"$SANDCASTLE_USER\"\n      ok \"Added '${SANDCASTLE_USER}' to group '${DOCKER_GROUP}'\"\n    fi\n  fi"
}
```

> AGENT

It's picking up `/home/thies/dockyard.env` from the current working dir (a different dockyard instance). The auto-discovery checks `./dockyard.env` before `../etc/dockyard.env`. Since there's a `dockyard.env` in the home directory, it picks that up first. This is expected — when running from the installed location (`/sandcastle/dockyard/bin/dockyard.sh`), the `../etc/dockyard.env` path resolves to `/sandcastle/dockyard/etc/dockyard.env`.

> AGENT

Everything is working cleanly. Here's a summary of what was done:

## Changes Made

### New Dockyard (upgraded from embedded to standalone)
- **Replaced** `installer/templates/dockyard.sh` with the latest version from `thieso2/dockyard`
- New dockyard is a single-script manager for Docker + Sysbox with per-instance isolation
- Single systemd unit (`sc_docker.service`) replaces the old two-service layout (`sc_sysbox` + `sc_docker`)
- Sysbox upgraded from 0.6.7 (nestybox) to 0.6.7.10-tc (patched fork with `--run-dir`)
- Per-instance user/group (`sc_docker:sc_docker`) for socket access control

### Directory Layout Change
| Old | New |
|---|---|
| `/sandcastle/docker-runtime/bin/docker` | `/sandcastle/dockyard/bin/docker` |
| `/sandcastle/docker.sock` | `/sandcastle/dockyard/run/docker.sock` |
| `/sandcastle/docker/` | `/sandcastle/dockyard/lib/docker` |
| `/sandcastle/sysbox/` | `/sandcastle/dockyard/lib/sysbox` |

### Files Modified
- **`installer/installer.sh.in`** — Updated `derive_vars()`, `setup_bashrc_path()`, `write_helper_scripts()`, `cmd_gen_env()`, `cmd_install()`, `cmd_destroy()` for new paths; added legacy cleanup
- **`installer/templates/dockyard.sh`** — Replaced with new standalone version
- **`installer/templates/sandcastle-admin.sh`** — Updated docker path with legacy fallback; fixed `work_dir` trap bug
- **`CLAUDE.md`** — Updated dockyard CLI paths

### Verified on Production
- Backup created from old install
- Old install fully uninstalled (clean slate)
- New install with `--from-backup` completed successfully
- Dockyard verify: all 6 checks pass (systemd, socket, API, containers, networking, DinD)
- Database restored: 1 user, 2 sandboxes
- Rails health check: 200 OK
- Backup/restore cycle works cleanly (no `work_dir` bug)

id: toolu_01XFkewXyt7qBv2DU8UEooD9
```
fix(sandbox): handle Sysbox UID remapping for bind-mounted directories

On kernel 6.17+, Sysbox containers cannot mkdir inside bind-mounted
directories owned by UIDs outside the container's user-namespace mapping,
even with 777 permissions. This caused SSH, VNC, and Tailscale to fail
when starting sandboxes with mounted home directories.

Fix by chowning bind-mounted dirs to root (UID 0, always mapped in
Sysbox) before container creation. Uses sudo chmod/chown since dirs may
be owned by a Sysbox-remapped UID from a previous container run. The
entrypoint is also hardened with || true guards so mkdir failures don't
crash-loop the container.

Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 4.6 <noreply@anthropic.com>
Entire-Checkpoint: 82f637d3ee6d
```

```
@@ -665,6 +661,20 @@ def container_runtime

+  # Prepare a directory for bind-mounting into Sysbox containers.
+  # Sets 777 mode and root ownership. On kernel 6.17+, Sysbox cannot create
+  # entries inside bind-mounted dirs owned by UIDs outside the container's
+  # user-namespace mapping (even with 777 mode). Host root (UID 0) is always
+  # mapped inside Sysbox. Uses sudo because the directory may already be owned
+  # by a Sysbox-remapped UID from a previous container run.
+  def prepare_bind_mount(path)
+    stat = File.stat(path)
+    system("/usr/bin/sudo", "-n", "/usr/bin/chmod", "777", path) unless stat.mode & 0o777 == 0o777
+    system("/usr/bin/sudo", "-n", "/usr/bin/chown", "0:0", path) unless stat.uid == 0
+  rescue Errno::ENOENT
+    # directory disappeared — race condition, ignore
+  end
+

     binds = []
     if sandbox.mount_home
```
